### 12.13 Verification results (Prompt 0)

**Read-only audit, 2026-09-26, Claude Code (Opus 5.5).** Every §12 issue checked against the code at these commits:
`gymsera_be` e237dc9 · `gyms_era` 809344b · `gymsera_cms` 8e18096 · `gymsera_web` 5b66fea.
Paths are relative to each repo (`be/` = `gymsera_be`, `app/` = `gyms_era`, `cms/` = `gymsera_cms`, `web/` = `gymsera_web`).

**Result values:** `CONFIRMED` (defect exists; file:line) · `PARTIAL` (some of the protection exists, the rest is missing; counted as CONFIRMED in totals) · `NOT REPRODUCED` (code already correct; file:line) · `NEEDS RUNTIME CHECK` (can't be decided from code alone; reason given).
Severity is the §12 severity unless the note says it was raised.

**Summary (164 §12 issues + new findings):**

| Result | Count | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| CONFIRMED | 98 | |
| PARTIAL (counted as confirmed) | 40 | |
| NOT REPRODUCED | 6 | AUTH-03, SEC-16, FLOW-13 fully; BILL-14, CAP-04, SEC-14 are split results (the named risk is absent, a related gap is confirmed) |
| NEEDS RUNTIME CHECK | 12 | CAP-08, FLOW-05, UX-06, UX-10, RT-03, RT-07, RT-09, PERF-01, PERF-02, PERF-11, GLB-06, OBS-04 (plus the Apple half of BILL-03, counted as CONFIRMED) |
| Decision rows (§14) | 4 | BILL-16, BILL-17, FLOW-11, GLB-07 |
| DEFERRED (R-13/R-14) | 4 | UX-03, UX-05, UX-16, UX-25 |
| **NEW** | **17** | 9 × P0 (NEW-01…08, NEW-15), 8 × P1 (NEW-09…14, NEW-16, NEW-BOOST) |

**Top risks, in the order Phase 1 should take them:** account takeover through social sign-in (NEW-02, NEW-03); unauthenticated maintenance/debug endpoints (NEW-01, NEW-04, NEW-05); free entitlement paths (NEW-15, NEW-06, NEW-07, BILL-04/08 unpaid states treated as ACTIVE, BILL-01 cross-account restore); cross-tenant access (SEC-02, NEW-08/SEC-01, RT-04); legacy RBAC still granting access (RBAC-07, AUTH-08).

#### 12.13.1 BILL

| ID | Result | Evidence | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| BILL-01 | CONFIRMED (worse than described) | `be/src/services/apple-billing.service.js:186-252`, `be/src/services/google-play-billing.service.js:133-184`, `be/src/services/stripe-billing.service.js:252-305`; client `app/lib/features/billing/presentation/providers/billing_provider.dart:249-262` | Lookup is by `externalOriginalTransactionId` only, never by tenant. `values.tenantId` is the **caller's** tenant and `existing.update(values)` writes it, so tenant B restoring tenant A's transaction **moves the row to B** (A silently loses its plan, no reconcile on A). No `appAccountToken`/`obfuscatedExternalAccountId` is sent (`PurchaseParam` has no `applicationUserName`) or checked. `external_original_transaction_id` is indexed, not unique (`be/src/models/platform/TenantSubscription.model.js:144`). |
| BILL-02 | PARTIAL | Apple `apple-billing.service.js:182,217` (`revocationDate` → `CANCELLED`); Google `google-play-billing.service.js:93-105` (no revoked/voided handling); Stripe `stripe-billing.service.js:393-442` (no `charge.refunded` / `charge.dispute.created`) | No `REVOKED` state (`TenantSubscription.model.js:99`). Apple refund is noticed only if the webhook carries it. Removing the ACTIVE row does **not** drop entitlement to 0: `resolveMaxBranches` falls back to `tenant.selectedPackageId` or `1` (`be/src/services/subscription-quota.service.js:46-50`). No Voided Purchases sweep. |
| BILL-03 | CONFIRMED (Android, Stripe); NEEDS RUNTIME CHECK (Apple) | Android `app/lib/features/billing/presentation/providers/billing_provider.dart:369-376` uses `ReplacementMode.withTimeProration` for upgrades **and** downgrades; Stripe `stripe-billing.service.js:176-180` `proration_behavior: 'create_prorations'` (immediate); server applies `plan.branchCount` of the verified product immediately (`apple-billing.service.js:197-202`) | Apple: a downgrade's signed transaction normally keeps the current product until renewal, so the immediate-apply risk depends on what Apple returns — verify in sandbox. No `pendingChange`, no "choose branches to keep". |
| BILL-04 | CONFIRMED | `TenantSubscription.model.js:98-102`; Google `google-play-billing.service.js:93-105` maps `ON_HOLD`, `PAUSED`, `PENDING` (and any unknown state) to **ACTIVE** via `default`; Stripe `stripe-billing.service.js:212-217` maps `past_due`, `incomplete`, `paused` to **ACTIVE** | Raised to **P0** in effect: an unpaid/on-hold subscription keeps full entitlement. Google `CANCELED` (auto-renew off, still paid to period end) maps to `CANCELLED`, which removes entitlement early. |
| BILL-05 | CONFIRMED | `apple-billing.service.js:195,214-216`; `google-play-billing.service.js:139,154-156`; `stripe-billing.service.js:258,277-279` | `amount` only written when `planChanged`; always the catalog price, never the provider's charged amount; no currency from the provider. |
| BILL-06 | PARTIAL | `be/src/controllers/billing.controller.js:132-139`; `google-play-billing.service.js:262-273` | Server-side acknowledge exists but is fire-and-forget after the response, not retried, and errors are only logged. RTDN path (`handleRtdnNotification`, `:282-300`) never acknowledges. If the app dies before `/sync`, nothing acknowledges and Play auto-refunds after 3 days. |
| BILL-07 | CONFIRMED | no `purchase-intent` route in `be/src/routes/billing.routes.js`; `subscription-migration.service.js:115-206` repairs afterwards | Only a Stripe-on-Stripe guard exists (`stripe-billing.service.js:117-120`). |
| BILL-08 | CONFIRMED (raised to P0) | server `google-play-billing.service.js:102-103` (`SUBSCRIPTION_STATE_PENDING` → ACTIVE, `paymentStatus: 'PAID'` at `:159`); client `billing_provider.dart:521-525` (pending shows the generic "purchasing" spinner) | A pending (not yet paid) Play purchase grants the plan immediately if the app syncs it. |
| BILL-09 | PARTIAL | `be/src/services/subscription-migration.service.js:285-298` | Resurrection is refused and a `statusNote` is written, but there is no `duplicateBilling` flag, no host banner, no admin flag, no email — only a server `console.warn`. |
| BILL-10 | CONFIRMED | Legacy sales still live: `be/src/controllers/host.controller.js:841-913` (host self-serve upgrade), `be/src/services/admin.service.js:675-782` (admin assign), `be/src/services/tenant-provisioning.service.js:510-545` (approval auto-subscription) — all create `platformPackageId` rows with no `billingPlanId`/`branchCount` | See also NEW-06 (the host upgrade grants any package for free). |
| BILL-11 | CONFIRMED | `be/src/models/platform/BillingPlan.model.js:49-51` (one `currency` column, default `PKR`) | Apps already prefer the store's localized price (`billing_provider.dart:209-214`). |
| BILL-12 | CONFIRMED | `be/src/controllers/billing.controller.js:102-109` (Apple: always 200, even on a transient DB error — the event is lost); no `BillingEvent` table (grep); `apple-billing.service.js:345-351` and `google-play-billing.service.js:293-298` drop notifications for unknown transactions; Apple uses the payload, not a refetch | No inbox, no dedupe beyond `CapacityEvent` keys, no sweep, no ordering protection. Google does refetch (`:289`). |

Not indefinite: the expiry cron suspends at `endDate` (`be/src/jobs/subscription-expiry.cron.js:145-170`). |

Web card payments are OFF by R-7 anyway. |

| CAP-03 | CONFIRMED | Four branch creators outside `createBranch`: admin `be/src/services/admin.service.js:1180-1264` (own copy of the capacity check, **no Tenant row lock**, so it races with host `createBranch` at `gym.service.js:445-451`); `be/src/services/membership-plan.service.js:201-211` (a **GET** `listForHost` creates an ACTIVE branch with no capacity check when the tenant has no plans and no branches); provisioning `be/src/services/tenant-provisioning.service.js:302-323` (re-approval flips an existing branch back to `ACTIVE` with no capacity event); new-org `be/src/controllers/host.controller.js:396-640` (own capacity copy, shares `_createBranchRecord`). Plus an unauthenticated mass `Branch.update` at `be/src/routes/discovery.routes.js:580-617` (NEW-01). | `moveBranch` (`gym.service.js:578-646`) and `deleteOrganization` (`:1127-1248`) run without a transaction or lock. |

But there is no separate `adminSuspended` state: an admin "disable" deletes the branch (credits capacity, cancels memberships). No audit row. |
| CAP-05 | CONFIRMED | New org is `PENDING` with an ACTIVE branch built immediately (`be/src/controllers/host.controller.js:542`, `:619`); reject only sets the listing to `REJECTED` (`be/src/services/admin.service.js:370-375`) | The branch stays ACTIVE and keeps consuming capacity; `getUsedCapacity` still counts the REJECTED listing's `reservedSlots` (it only excludes INACTIVE, `subscription-quota.service.js:102`). |
| CAP-06 | CONFIRMED | `be/src/services/gym.service.js:1027-1029` | Restore into an INACTIVE org returns 409 ("move it instead") instead of reactivating the org; the org's preserved `reservedSlots` become inert (`subscription-quota.service.js:98-102`). |

| CAP-08 | NEEDS RUNTIME CHECK | Host `createBranch` locks the Tenant row (`gym.service.js:445-451`); delete/restore lock the branch row (`:817`, `:1010`) | Code looks serialised for host paths; admin create (above) does not take the Tenant lock. Needs the parallel test. |

#### 12.13.3 FLOW

#### 12.13.4 PAY

| ID | Result | Evidence | Notes |

Two concurrent requests with the same key both pass the `findOne` and the second hits the unique index → 500, not a replay. Payment + membership activation are not one transaction (`payment.service.js:101-155`). |

Currency hard-coded `'PKR'` in several creators. |
| PAY-03 | CONFIRMED | There is no ledger-entry table: the ledger is computed from `payments` rows, which are updated in place (`payment.service.js:265`, `:429`, `:450`, `:550-563`, `:577`) | Verifying/rejecting an old payment changes the history of a past business day. Adjustments are a separate append-only table (`be/src/services/ledger.service.js:274-314`). |
| PAY-04 | PARTIAL | Business date in branch timezone (`ledger.service.js:36-64`); close is race-safe and one-shot (`:316-366`) | Step 2.7: Payment `business_date` is set once from collection time and made strictly immutable via model hooks (`Payment.model.js`). `verifyPayment`, `markPrinted`, `uploadPaymentProof`, and `markPaymentFailed` never shift `business_date`. Updates attempting to mutate `business_date` are rejected. Migration 006 enforces `business_date DATE NOT NULL` conditionally. Step 2.8: Single authority `getPaymentCollectionTime` in `ledger.service.js` unifies fallback rule (`collected_at` -> `created_at` for CASH; `paid_at` for ONLINE/BANK_TRANSFER) across model hooks, Migration 004, and Query B. Step 2.9: Added maintenance repair script (`src/scripts/repair-payment-business-dates.js`) with preview-by-default (read-only transaction), `--apply --confirm` safety guard, CLOSED ledger day protection ("needs manual adjustment"), tenant-level transactions, and audit logging to `audit_logs` with before/after state via `allowBusinessDateRepair` hook bypass. Step 2.11: Refined `getPaymentCollectionTime` to select EARLIER of created_at and paid_at for CASH without collected_at (normal rows keep created_at; imported rows with import-time created_at use paid_at); non-cash pending payments finalize provisional business_date automatically on first completion via payment service with `fromPaymentServiceTransition: true`; ordinary updates remain immutable. Note: `addAdjustment` refusal on CLOSED day remains for Phase 1. |
| PAY-05 | CONFIRMED | Three copies of a random `INV-YYYYMMDD-<6 hex>` generator: `payment.service.js:10`, `gym.service.js:9`, `subscription.service.js:48` | Not sequential, not gapless, collisions possible, not per branch. |
| PAY-06 | PARTIAL | `staffCollectedBy`/`createdBy` recorded (`Payment.model.js:78`, `payment.service.js:547-563`) | No shift concept; daily close shows totals, not cash per collector vs expected. |

| PAY-09 | PARTIAL | `app/lib/core/services/printer_service.dart` (no "DUPLICATE" marker, no raster rendering for Urdu/Arabic) | `printed_at` is tracked server-side (`TenantDbManager.js:133-138`). |

| ID | Result | Evidence | Notes |

| AUTH-01 | PARTIAL | `be/src/services/auth.service.js:642-672` rotates on use | Refresh tokens are JWTs stored **in plain text** (`be/src/models/platform/RefreshToken.model.js:16`); reusing a revoked token just returns 401 — no reuse detection, no session-family revoke. |
| AUTH-02 | CONFIRMED | No session list/revoke and **no logout endpoint** in `be/src/routes/auth.routes.js` (grep "logout" finds nothing) | Only password reset revokes all refresh tokens (`auth.service.js:744`). |
| AUTH-03 | NOT REPRODUCED | Mobile single-flight `Completer` (`app/lib/core/network/error_interceptor.dart:13-80`); CMS/web `isRefreshing` + subscriber queue (`cms/src/lib/api/client.ts:21-71`, `web/src/lib/api/client.ts:21-71`) | Web/CMS never reject queued subscribers when the refresh fails (they hang); minor. |
| AUTH-04 | CONFIRMED | 6-digit codes, 10-minute expiry (`be/src/utils/otp.utils.js:7-20`) but stored in plain text and matched by DB equality (`auth.service.js:32-40`); no attempt counter (grep `attempt` in `Otp.model.js`/`auth.service.js` finds nothing); auth limiter is 1000 requests / 15 min per IP (`be/app.js:156-173`) | Brute force of a 6-digit code is feasible; no per-identifier limit or lockout. |
| AUTH-05 | PARTIAL | One backend verifier with an `aud` allow-list, used by mobile, web and CMS (`auth.service.js:324-386`; `web/src/lib/api/auth.ts:51`; CMS uses `/auth/social/google/staff`) | But the verifier has an unsigned-token fallback (NEW-03) and links accounts by e-mail. |
| AUTH-06 | CONFIRMED (**P0**) | `auth.service.js:518-560` | Apple identity token is only `jwt.decode`d — **signature never verified**, `aud` computed but not enforced (`:536`, `:539`), `appleId` and e-mail may come from the client (`:548-549`) and an existing account is linked by e-mail (`:567-580`). Anyone can sign in as any user. Same flaw in re-auth (`:831-844`). See NEW-02. |
| AUTH-07 | CONFIRMED | `be/src/services/me.service.js:439-447` | "Request deletion" only sets `users.status = INACTIVE`. No tenant deletion, no store-subscription preflight, no re-auth, no undo window, no data deletion, no Apple token revoke, no device-token cleanup. |
| AUTH-08 | PARTIAL (P0 in effect) | `can()` resolves grants from the DB per request with version-keyed cache (`be/src/services/access.service.js:176-200`) | Legacy `authorize()` routes use the **JWT role**, and `applyLegacyRoleShim` returns early when the user has no live assignment (`be/src/middleware/tenantContext.js:67`), so a revoked team member whose `users.role` was set to `BRANCH_MANAGER` keeps access to every `/gyms/*` route (`be/src/routes/gyms.routes.js:13`) until they leave the tenant context. |
| AUTH-09 | CONFIRMED | `be/src/services/admin.service.js:14-34` | Links an existing account by e-mail and upgrades its role to `GYM_HOST`, or creates a **verified** account with a random password; no invitation, no audit. `team.service.js:245-266` does the same for team invites. |
| AUTH-10 | PARTIAL | Reset code is single-use, 10 minutes, and revokes refresh tokens (`auth.service.js:718-747`) | Code stored in plain text, no attempt limit (see AUTH-04). |
| RBAC-01 | PARTIAL | Grantor cap enforced (`access.service.js:367-386`); preset tiers are not written by overrides | The save is a full overwrite (`PUT /team/:id/permissions` → `be/src/services/team.service.js:428-470` deletes all overrides and re-inserts), not `changes[]`; a client that omits a row silently removes it. |
| RBAC-02 | CONFIRMED | Backend role name "Support" (`be/src/constants/roles.js:116-118`); mobile shows "Cleaner" (`app/lib/features/host/presentation/screens/team_access_screen.dart:16`, `:209`) | No shared `roleLabels` table; the CMS has no team screen yet. P2. |
| RBAC-03 | CONFIRMED | `be/tests/*.test.js` are hand-written scenarios against a **live** server (`be/tests/helpers.js:1-12`) | No generated endpoint × persona matrix. |
| RBAC-04 | PARTIAL | Approver re-checked at decision time, race-safe claim, command re-validated (`be/src/services/approval.service.js:161-240`) | Requester's current access is not re-checked; command execution is not in a transaction with the claim — a command that half-succeeds and then throws is reset to PENDING (`:241-250`) and can execute again. |
| RBAC-05 | PARTIAL | Level rule on invite and update (`access.service.js:338-360`; `team.service.js:208`, `:360`) | There is no acceptance step: `/team/invites` creates an **ACTIVE** assignment immediately (`team.service.js:286-300`), so "re-check at acceptance" can't exist. |
| RBAC-06 | CONFIRMED | Admin routes guarded only by `authorize('PLATFORM_ADMIN')` (`be/src/routes/admin.routes.js`) | No sub-roles; `be/src/middleware/auditLog.js` exists but see SEC-12. |
| RBAC-07 | CONFIRMED (**P0**) | Legacy access sources still live: `GymStaff.designation === 'admin'` grants payments/expenses access (`be/src/controllers/payments.controller.js:35-45`, `be/src/controllers/expenses.controller.js:97`); legacy staff CRUD `/gyms/staff` → `createStaffUser` (`be/src/services/gym.service.js:1707-1760`, sets global `users.role = 'BRANCH_MANAGER'`); `/staff-invites/*` (`be/src/routes/staff-invites.routes.js:95-106`); CMS `/gym/staff` page | The legacy shim maps **any** live assignment to `BRANCH_MANAGER` (`tenantContext.js:69-75`), so a Cleaner passes every `authorize('GYM_HOST','BRANCH_MANAGER')` route — e.g. `PATCH /gyms/profile` (change the gym's payment details) and `PATCH /gyms/branches/:id` for any branch. |
| RBAC-08 | CONFIRMED | `team.service.js:428-470` | No `expectedVersion`; last write wins (the code comment claims PUT prevents this; it does not). |
| RBAC-09 | CONFIRMED | `deleteBranch` terminates `GymStaff` only (`gym.service.js:860-865`); `RoleAssignmentBranch` links survive | After `restoreBranch` the old branch-scoped assignments grant access again automatically. |

No secret scanning in CI (there is no CI). |

| TEST-FLAKE-1A (intermittent full-suite failure reported after Prompt 1A) | RESOLVED (mechanism not proven) | Symptom: in ~3 of 14 full `npm test` runs one test failed that always passed on its own, a different test each time (`harness.test.js`; BILL-14 session check got 400; BILL-06 RTDN ack not called). Believed cause: real outbound network calls during tests — `tests/integration/tenant-provisioning-migrations.test.js` sent a real approval e-mail through `src/services/email.service.js` → `mail.gymsera.com:587` (and one test run hit the real Google Play API before provider credentials were blanked). A real SMTP send takes a variable, sometimes long, time and its connection/timers are not awaited by the test that started it; with `--runInBand` all files share one process, so the unfinished call keeps running into the NEXT test file — during its setup (tables truncated/recreated) or its first requests — and whichever file happens to be running when it lands is the one disturbed. That explains why a different test failed each time and why every failing test passed alone | Network jail `tests/harness/no-network.js` (jest `setupFiles`) blocking connections and all DNS resolution except localhost; fake mail sender `tests/harness/mail-fake.js` via the new `email.service.js#mailTransport` seam (same pattern as billing's `appleApi`/`playApi`/`stripeApi`) | Evidence: with outbound e-mail blocked, 10/10 consecutive full runs passed (95/95, 20/20 suites), then 5/5 more (96/96, 20/20) after the DNS gap was closed and the provisioning test switched to the fake sender, with zero real e-mails and zero blocked attempts in those runs. We did not capture the failing interaction itself, so this is RESOLVED on consistent removal of the symptom, not a proven root cause — reopen if it recurs | `gymsera_be/tests/regression/no-outbound-network.test.js`, `gymsera_be/tests/integration/tenant-provisioning-migrations.test.js` | be bed0855 (network block) + DNS/mail-fake commit on `phase-1/prompt-1a-billing-core` | n/a (test infrastructure) |

| TEST-FLAKE-1B (intermittent full-suite failure, 1A + 1B) | DONE — cause proven (supersedes the "mechanism not proven" note on TEST-FLAKE-1A) | supertest 7.3 starts a bare Express app with `app.listen(0)` on **every address (::)** and then calls **127.0.0.1** (`node_modules/supertest/lib/test.js:90`, `:105`). Evidence: 15 baseline runs → runs 3 and 4 failed in different tests (BILL-06 `/android/sync` got **403** with no `[Audit]` line for it — the app never saw the request; BILL-03 got **"socket hang up"**); a detectOpenHandles run failed a third test (BILL-04 resumed RTDN not applied — the webhook's response is not checked). Port stress on this machine: 3 of 5000 `listen(0)` ports were ports held on 127.0.0.1 by other programs; a controlled repro (`tests/regression/test-server-loopback.test.js`) shows 127.0.0.1 answered by the other program. | BUILD-PROD-DEPS (web + CMS) | DONE | Both Next.js apps: `tsconfig.json` had `"include": ["**/*.ts", …]` with only `node_modules` excluded, so `next build` type-checked test-only files (`playwright.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, `e2e/`, `tests/`) and failed wherever `@playwright/test`/`vitest` were absent; and the build-time tools (`typescript`, `@types/node`, `@types/react`, `@types/react-dom`, `tailwindcss`, `postcss`, `autoprefixer`) were `devDependencies`, so a prod-only install (`npm ci --omit=dev`) could not build at all (CMS repro on a clean copy of main: `Cannot find module 'autoprefixer'`, and every `@/` import unresolved because Next reads tsconfig `paths` through `typescript`) | Same fix in both repos; lockfile versions unchanged (only the `dev` flags move) | `tsconfig.json` `exclude` adds `playwright.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, `e2e`, `tests`, `test-results`, `playwright-report` (vitest/playwright still load their own files, so tests are unaffected); the seven build-time packages above moved to `dependencies`. **Rule for any repo:** everything `npm run build` needs (compiler, type packages, CSS/PostCSS toolchain) belongs in `dependencies`; test runners and their configs must stay out of the build's type-check. Check with `npm ci --omit=dev && npm run build` on a clean checkout, and delete any devDependency npm pulls in anyway (`next` drags `@playwright/test` in as a peer) before building. Tests and CI unchanged (CI still runs a full `npm ci`) | — (build check: clean `npm ci --omit=dev` + `npm run build` passes in both repos with no devDependencies present; CMS vitest passes) | web 12c9914, cms a99547e | n/a (build) — the next production deploy of each app is the real check |

| NEW-03 | DONE | `be/src/services/auth.service.js:494-523` (at 93587ab, added in 60b0c83): when `googleClient.verifyIdToken` threw and the error text contained "timed out" / "certificates" / "ECONNREFUSED" / "ETIMEDOUT" / "ENOTFOUND" / "network", the token was `jwt.decode`d (**no signature check**) and accepted if iss/aud/exp/sub/email looked right. Reachable by anyone: the library echoes the token header in its "No pem found for envelope …" error, so a `kid` containing "network" triggers it; a failed or slow cert fetch did too. Result: sign in (or pass re-auth) as any Google user, including via the CMS staff route. Also `email_verified: "false"` (string) passed the check (`:526`) | `google-auth-library` `OAuth2Client#verifyIdToken` (already the primary path; kept), `createError` + `errorHandler` 401 shape (same as every auth failure), SEC-07 redaction for the log line | Fallback removed. Only the first line of the verifier's error is logged. Applies to `googleLogin` (app/web and CMS staff) and `verifyReauthCredential` (they share `_verifyGoogleIdToken`) | `gymsera_be/tests/regression/new-03-google-token-verification.test.js` (19 tests; Google's certs replaced by a local test key via `OAuth2Client#getFederatedSignonCertsAsync`, no network). Still open: no recorded step or lock — `approvableStatuses` includes APPROVED (`be/src/services/admin.service.js:372`) and `processTenantProvisioning` ran inline with no lock (`:388`), so two approvals both ran (proven: the second failed with a DDL race `Duplicate key name 'branches_gym_id'` → the admin saw 502 although the tenant became ACTIVE); find-then-create with no lock for listing/gym/branch (`tenant-provisioning.service.js:226,273,308`); a failed GymListing insert was swallowed (`:257-260`) → proven: tenant ACTIVE with no listing and a branch linked to none; the legacy plan step ran after ACTIVE and swallowed errors (`:577-579`) → proven: tenant ACTIVE with 0 plan rows; slot attribution swallowed (`:403-405`); no Idempotency-Key on the route (`be/src/routes/admin.routes.js:108`); no resume/sweep | Platform migration runner (p013, same pattern as p005/p006/p012; dry-run + conflict + re-run tests); REL-01 `idempotency.js` middleware and `idempotency_records` as is (optional key, like PAY-01 but not required so the mobile admin keeps working); the existing daily sweep `subscription-expiry.cron.js#runExpiryCheck` (also the Vercel cron) — no new job system; `recordCapacityEvent` idempotency key; natural-key find-or-create already in the steps; `log-redaction.js#redactString` for the stored error; the seam pattern (`mailTransport`/`playApi`) for failure injection | p013 adds `tenants.provisioning_state` / `provisioning_lock_token` / `provisioning_locked_until` / `provisioning_error` (all NULL, no backfill; all-or-nothing: a conflicting column skips the whole migration). Provisioning is six recorded steps `DB_CREATED → MODELS_SYNCED → LISTING_CREATED → BRANCH_CREATED → SUBSCRIPTION_LINKED → ACTIVE` (approve first records `REQUESTED`, only if nothing is recorded). A run takes a 10-minute lease on the Tenant row by a conditional UPDATE (only on an APPROVED tenant with no live lease), renews it at every step, and every write checks the token + APPROVED, so a run whose lease was taken over, or a tenant rejected meanwhile, stops without activating. A second approve while a run holds the lease changes nothing and returns `provisioning.inProgress` (HTTP 202 with the step). A failure releases the lease, keeps the last finished step and stores the (redacted, ≤500 chars) error; Resume = approve again, continues from there. The listing and plan steps no longer swallow errors; plan (`planForApproval` + legacy package plan) and slot attribution moved into step 5 (still before ACTIVE and before slots are read); slot event + `reservedSlots` in one platform transaction. Activation is the step-6 write itself (status ACTIVE + dbName + connection string + lease cleared); e-mail/notification once, after it. A resumed run settles the DB host the same way step 1 does, then logs in once as the app user (R-25 unchanged). The daily cron resumes up to 5 stalled runs (state recorded, lease free); tenants APPROVED before 1G (no state) are left for the admin. `GET /admin/tenants/:id` returns `tenant.provisioning {state, step, totalSteps, inProgress, lockedUntil, lastError, canResume}`; the lock token never leaves the server (`Tenant#toJSON`). Route takes an optional `Idempotency-Key`. CMS tenant page: panel "Provisioning… (step n/6)" / "stopped at step n/6" with last error and **Resume**, polls every 10 s while in progress, one Idempotency-Key per click. **Read-only check** `gymsera-flow02-provisioning-check.js` (READ ONLY transactions; works before and after p013; needs explicit `CHK_USER`/`CHK_PASSWORD`): STUCK_APPROVED_NO_DB / STUCK_APPROVED_PARTIAL / ACTIVE_NO_CONNECTION / ACTIVE_NO_LISTING / ACTIVE_NO_PLAN / DUPLICATE_GYM / BRANCH_WITHOUT_LISTING / DUPLICATE_LISTING_AT_APPROVAL / UNREACHABLE_TENANT_DB / ORPHAN_DATABASE. **Verdict definitions corrected 2026-10-01** (the first run on the live DB reported DUPLICATE_GYM=10 and DUPLICATE_LISTING_AT_APPROVAL=4, false positives): the first version flagged any tenant DB with more than one `gyms` row and any tenant whose two oldest listings were created within 5 minutes. Both are normal: adding an organization creates its own GymListing **and** its own Gym row (`be/src/controllers/host.controller.js:534`, `:598-613`), and seed data inserts a tenant's listings in the same second (`be/src/seeders/seed.js:1023-1027`). Now **DUPLICATE_GYM** = two or more gyms share one `gym_listing_id`, or there are at least two gyms and more gyms than the tenant has listings; **DUPLICATE_LISTING_AT_APPROVAL** = two listings of one tenant with the same title (trimmed, case-insensitive) created within 5 minutes of each other, any pair, not only the two oldest. Titles are compared, never printed; the output line also shows `gymsSharingListing` | `gymsera_be/tests/regression/flow-02-resumable-provisioning.test.js` (20: crash after each of the 6 steps' work before it is recorded → Resume → exactly one DB, listing, gym, branch, membership plan, subscription row and capacity event, reservedSlots 1; failure before a step + admin summary step 3/6 without the token; real failures inside steps (branch, listing, plan insert); approval e-mail once; two approvals at once → one runs, one in-progress; live lease → nothing done; expired lease → taken over; lease taken over mid-run → stops, not ACTIVE; HTTP Idempotency-Key replay; HTTP 202 + GET summary; sweep resumes a stalled run and leaves a pre-1G APPROVED tenant alone; cron calls the sweep; resume into a half-made general_ci database → finished, collations joinable, schema at target). Added 2026-10-01 (21st test): provisioning and a resumed provisioning with a tenant app user that has **no server-wide privileges** (USAGE only; before this the app user was `ALL ON *.*` locally and root in CI) — both finish, the user can read only its own tenant database, not the platform database or another tenant's. #### Prompt 3A notes (CMS Team & Access + Approvals, 2026-10-06)

Acceptance (§8.3.7): the CMS reads a person's effective permissions from the same response as the app (`GET /team/:assignmentId` →
`effectivePermissions`) and applies the same rule (`cms/src/lib/team/access.ts#savedTier` = mobile `TeamMemberDetail.tierFor`).
`cms/tests/api/team.test.ts` checks the two rules give the same tier for every permission of four sample people.
Not verified against a running backend or a device: all CMS tests mock the API.

Backend behaviour and where the CMS handles it:

| Behaviour | Team page | Approvals page |
|---|---|---|
| `expectedVersion` / `409 grants_changed` (RBAC-08) | Sent on both edits; reload + notice | not applicable (no versioned edit) |
| 403 level rule (RBAC-05) | Roles disabled from `assignableByMe`; panel locked for a member at or above the actor's level and for the actor's own record; server sentence shown on 403 | 403 on the inbox → "Your requests" only; 403 on a decision → reason + reload |
| 202 on approval-tier actions | none of the `/team` routes answer 202 (team permissions are not approvable) | helper + notice built; nothing in the CMS triggers a 202 yet |
| Re-auth | none of the `/team` or `/approvals` routes call `assertReauth` | same |

Differences found between the app and the CMS (owner to decide; none changes what the server allows):

1. **Preset-only tiers.** The CMS shows View / Approve / Full preset rows as read-only labels (this spec, §8.3.3). The mobile editor shows a
   switch on those rows, so the app can turn a preset "view" permission off for one person and the CMS cannot (the CMS keeps such a row
   editable only when an override already exists). Either the mobile editor gets the labels, or the CMS gets the switch back.
2. **Mobile has no RBAC-08 handling.** `team_repository.dart:138-149` sends no `expectedVersion`, so a save from the app still overwrites
   a concurrent change silently; the server only checks the version when it is sent.
3. **Mobile save drops `constraints`.** `PermissionOverride.toJson` (`team_models.dart:368-373`) omits it and the save replaces the whole set.
   The CMS sends back every saved override unchanged, including `constraints`.
4. **Mobile "Reset to defaults" cannot be saved**: the save bar is shown only while staged overrides exist (`permission_editor_screen.dart:88`).
   The mobile diff sheet lists raw permission keys, lists every saved override (not only the changed ones), and labels an on/off permission
   "Needs approval" (`:520-532`). The CMS diff shows labels, only changed rows, before → after.
5. **Role labels (RBAC-02 still open).** Both clients show the server's `role.name` ("Support", "Branch Manager", "Org Admin", "Front Desk"),
   not the §8.3.2 labels ("Cleaner", "Manager"). No shared `roleLabels` table exists on the backend, so the CMS did not invent one.
6. **Role and branch change** exists in the CMS panel (`PATCH /team/:id`); the mobile repository has the call (`:110-132`) but no screen uses it.
7. **Not ported in 3A:** per-branch staff requests and web invite acceptance (UX-22), `POST /approvals/:id/collect` (mobile workspace only),
   suspend / reactivate (`status` on `PATCH /team/:id`; no mobile screen either), the audit list (`GET /team/audit`).
8. `grants_changed` is not in the shared error-copy table (§4.2) on the backend or in any client; the CMS writes its own sentence for it.

The 2026-10-14 failure that opened NEW-83 was a `beforeEach` 30 s timeout in a full run and nobody checked the sleep log then, so **the likely cause is the laptop sleeping, not Redis.** Not proven. | SEC-16 (Prompt 5A-4, PR 6) | DONE (hardening). The injection half stays NOT REPRODUCED. Verified in staging: no | **Injection: not reproduced.** Search text reaches MySQL as a bound value (Sequelize `Op.like`), and the only raw SQL outside `src/database/` interpolates constants or a validated database name. **Found instead:** the five user searches put the raw text into the LIKE pattern, so `%` or `_` matched every row and a long run of them made MySQL do needless work: `user.service.js:69-71`, `gym.service.js:1628-1630`, `host.controller.js:1107-1109`, `audit.service.js:80` (action prefix), and the pay-later prefix in `subscription-expiry.cron.js:231` | the SEC-04 / SEC-08 shape: one shared helper plus a source guard | `utils/sql-like.js` (`likeContains`, `likeStartsWith`, `likeEndsWith`): escapes `%` and `_`, caps the term at 100 characters, takes the first value of a repeated parameter and treats objects as empty. **Backslashes are dropped, not escaped:** in this MySQL 5.7 setup an escaped backslash did not match reliably (measured), and no name, e-mail or phone has one. All five sites use it. `tests/regression/sec-16-sql-guard.test.js` fails if a LIKE pattern is built any other way, or if `query` / `literal` interpolates anything outside a reviewed list (4 entries, each with a reason; stale entries fail too). `DROP DATABASE \`${dbName}\`` in `orphan-database.service.js` is on that list: `SAFE_NAME` is checked first and it is a CLI tool. Fix: `POST /tenants/:id/reapply` (`tenantService.reapplyTenant`): owner only, only from REJECTED (else 409 `not_rejected`), status → DRAFT, all entered data kept, rejection reason kept visible until the next review, `kycStatus` PENDING when files are still held or NOT_SUBMITTED when the 90-day sweep (R-16) already removed them, audit `tenant.reapplied`. Not done: (a) refund hook: nothing is charged at registration (web card OFF, R-7; pay-later has no charge), so there is nothing to refund until R-4 setup-mode is built; (b) CMS already shows the reason; the web wizard has no rejected screen; (c) a stricter rate limit on the route |

#### Prompt 4A close-out notes (2026-10-14)

**How it was measured.** Local MySQL 5.7 (the `gymsera_test_*` databases only, R-19); the production database was never queried. A throwaway harness counted Sequelize queries per request and timed 5 reads per route against N seeded ACTIVE tenants (tenants share one tenant database, so query counts and pool counts are real, payload sizes are not). Baseline before 4A, 100 tenants: discovery 305 queries per read (p50 180 ms), cold first read 2.9 s; one pool per tenant (100); 5 platform queries before any handler on a tenant request (10 for a team member); `/admin/tenants` 52 queries.

**Operational notes (read before deploying).**
- **No migration** in 4A (only `TenantDbManager.js` changed under `be/src/database`).
- **Invalidation is single-instance.** The discovery snapshot and memos are in process: a hide / suspend / delete is immediate on the instance that handled it, and reaches another instance within the 60 s TTL. Owner decision (2026-10-14): one instance, no platform-DB invalidation row. If a second API instance is ever run, add one (a platform table version read per request) first.
- **Cloudflare.** The API is behind Cloudflare: `content-encoding: gzip` is already applied and `cf-cache-status: DYNAMIC`, so no compression package was added. Discovery GETs now send `public, s-maxage=60`; if the zone caches API paths, a hidden gym can be served from the edge for up to 60 s (`DISCOVERY_CDN_MAX_AGE_S`, 0 sends `no-store`).
- **Pool ceiling.** 200 pools x 3 connections = 600 connections per API process, soft cap. Size MySQL `max_connections` for it (§16 already lists this) and watch `getMetrics()`.
- **304 saves bandwidth only.** The handler and its queries still run. Mobile dio does not send `If-None-Match`; browsers revalidate on their own.
- **`/me/bootstrap` needs a device test** (see not measured). Feature flags, min app version and error-copy version are NEW-81.

**Not measured / not verified in 4A.** Device cold start (budget 2.5 s) and real request counts on a session (code reading only); Lighthouse for web; production or staging p95 (offline sandbox, and prohibited against production); the 200 RPS discovery load test and the 1,000-tenant pool soak test named in §12.10; tab flicker, `/host` redirect and deep-link timing on a device; the real deployed `/me/bootstrap` from the app. Everything above ran against local test databases and stubbed clients.

**Observed, not scheduled (promote to NEW-83 and up if wanted).**
- `admin.service.js` `updateBranchTravelerVisibility` and `getBranchVisibilityHistory` scan tenants one by one for a branch id (not list routes); they could reuse the "snapshot's tenant first" lookup from PERF-06.
- `membership.service.js#resolveTenantForBranch` scans every ACTIVE tenant DB on a cache miss (a Redis hit avoids it).
- `claimInvitesForUser` queries the tenant DB on every tenant request.
- `NotificationService.initialize` is awaited before `runApp` in `gyms_era/lib/main.dart` (push, out of 4A scope).

**Process note.** The B7 full-suite verification was interrupted twice by the removal of the agent's scratch worktree (by mistake, owner). The first attempt's runs 2 and 3 failed with `ENOENT` on files inside the worktree. The repeat, from the surviving branch, gave runs 1 and 2 green (161 suites, 1503/1503 tests each) and a third run interrupted the same way (306 `ENOENT`, no test failure). CI passed 4/4 on PR #105. A run that fails with `ENOENT` inside the worktree says nothing about the code.

#### Prompt 5A-2 close-out notes (2026-10-16)

**Scope.** RT-01 to RT-09 (realtime and notifications), plus NEW-84, NEW-84b and NEW-85 (found while doing RT-04 and RT-08), NEW-83 re-test, and NEW-86 (found while verifying NEW-85). Backend PRs #109 to #114 are merged; NEW-84b (`167505b`) and NEW-85 (`5435ae1`) are committed on their own branches and not yet merged. Mobile PRs #9 to #15 are merged. Nothing here was run against staging or a device.

**How it was verified.** Every fix was failing-first. Backend: the full suite three times plus once with `DISABLE_REDIS=true` (last counts: 176 suites / 1576 tests). Mobile: `flutter test` three times, `flutter analyze` with no new findings against the master baseline. **Process note:** two NEW-85 full runs were invalidated because the laptop slept overnight (wall times of 25,746 s and 29,698 s, all failures were 30 s hook timeouts, and the same code passed before and after). They were replaced by two clean runs under `caffeinate -i`; the logs were kept. Use `caffeinate -i` for long local runs.

**Deploy notes (read before deploying).**
- **Two platform migrations: p020 and p021. Apply both before the backend build that uses them goes live.** Check first with `node src/scripts/run-platform-migrations.js --dry-run`, then run it without the flag. Then confirm `schema_migrations` holds versions 20 and 21 (platform "at v21") and that the columns exist.
- **p020** (`p020_add_device_token_context`, RT-01): `device_tokens.app_version`, `locale`, `timezone`. Nullable, no backfill, safe to re-run. **p020 was missed once** during the 5A-2 deploys (reported by the owner; it was applied afterwards, and the details of what failed in between were not recorded here). Registration now writes these columns, so a build running without p020 is expected to fail on token registration (reasoned from the model, not observed). After any deploy, check that `device_tokens` has the three columns before trusting push registration.
- **p021** (`p021_add_message_client_message_id`, RT-05): `messages.client_message_id` plus UNIQUE (`conversation_id`, `sender_id`, `client_message_id`). The `Message` model selects the column, so **without p021 every message read fails with "Unknown column"**: apply it first. It skips (and reports) instead of failing if the column exists with another type or if duplicate keys already exist. Backup before applying: `mysqldump <platform_db> messages > messages_pre_p021.sql`. Rollback: deploy the previous app build first, then drop the index and column and delete the `schema_migrations` row for version 21 (the exact statements are in the migration header).
- **Mobile builds need no migration** and tolerate an older backend: `clientMessageId` is ignored by an old server, a missing `unreadCount` falls back to a re-fetch, and `grants.changed` simply never arrives.
- **NEW-84 / 84b / 85 change who receives what.** Staff receive `new_message`, `ledger_updated` and `conversation_updated` only for their branches (owners and org-wide staff receive every branch). Because of NEW-86 this currently reaches owners only; see below.

**Open decision for the owner: NEW-86.** Staff sockets get no tenant or branch rooms because the gateway trusts a token claim that staff tokens do not carry. Decide whether sockets should derive their tenants from membership. Until then, staff rely on push and refetch, as before 5A-2.

**P3 follow-ups (not scheduled).**
1. Drop a notification meant for a different user on the same device (spec §2.10 resolver step 2; the tap handler never checks the notification's user).
2. Refresh the router when `grants.changed` arrives (the host-route redirect decides from auth and staff status and only re-evaluates when those change).
3. Traveler inbox spinner flash on reconnect (`TravelerInboxNotifier.load` sets a loading state before it fetches).
4. Stop retrying on an expired token (with unlimited reconnect attempts, an expired token keeps retrying at the 30 s cap until the next app resume refreshes it).

**Not done in 5A-2.** Server-side replay by `lastEventId` (RT-06 uses client refetch, which the spec allows); host-owner providers (`myTenantProvider`, `hostBranchesProvider`) are not invalidated on `grants.changed`; `authorizeConversation` still uses `userBelongsToTenant` (it requires role `GYM_HOST`, so a member cannot reach it); the `UserGymMembership` branch of `userBelongsToTenant` is unchanged for HTTP routes.

#### Prompt 5A-3 close-out notes (2026-10-19)

**Scope.** OBS-01 to OBS-07 and OBS-09 on the backend (PRs #119 to #126; #122 is a one-line lint fix), OBS-01 on the CMS (PR #28), web (PR #17) and mobile (PR #16), and the OBS-05 view in the CMS (PR #29). **Not done: OBS-02 on the CMS, web and mobile (PR9, waits for a Sentry DSN from the owner) and OBS-08 client performance (PR12).** Decisions R-32 to R-34 (§14) are recorded. **Nothing here was run against staging, production, a real Sentry project, a Prometheus server or a device.**

**How it was verified.** Every backend change was failing-first, and each new guard was mutation-checked (counts in the rows). Full backend suite, three normal runs plus one `DISABLE_REDIS=true` run: OBS-01 179 suites / 1610 tests, OBS-06 181 / 1630, OBS-05 185 / 1656. OBS-03: one run whose file pattern matched every suite (188 / 1707). OBS-02: a final full run at Node's default heap (190 / 1743). For OBS-07 and OBS-04, and for the client request-id work, the owner agreed a lighter protocol: new tests plus related suites (OBS-07 54 suites / 442 tests; OBS-04 35 / 356, also with `SLOW_QUERY_MS=1`), the rest left to GitHub CI. CMS (OBS-05): vitest 57 files / 423 tests, Playwright 26/26, `tsc` and lint clean, `npm run build` exit 0.

**Process notes (read before the next long local run).**
1. **Laptop sleep invalidates runs.** Two OBS-06 no-Redis runs failed (3 suites / 33 tests; then 2 suites with durations of 1,790 s and 3,566 s). The power log (`pmset -g log`) showed lid-closed sleep inside both windows, the same suites passed alone, and a clean re-run on AC power with the lid open was green. `caffeinate -i` does not stop clamshell sleep on battery. Keep the lid open and the charger in, and check `pmset -g log` for Sleep and Wake lines inside the run window before trusting a failure. This is the NEW-83 hypothesis.
2. **Jest runs previously failed suites first**, so the order changes after a failure; a test that depends on order shows up as "fails only sometimes".
3. **A virtual jest mock of an installed package can let the real package through** (order and cache dependent). Mock normally once the package is installed.
4. **Measuring heap per suite.** `--logHeapUsage` prints nothing for passing suites with the default reporter here, and `--json` has no `memoryUsage`. Use a small custom reporter that calls `global.gc()` in `onTestResult` and reads `process.memoryUsage()` (run in band it executes inside the test process), with `node --expose-gc ./node_modules/.bin/jest --runInBand`.

**Test hygiene rule (new).** Anything process-level that outlives a test file keeps that file's whole module graph (app, routes, models: about 22 MB) alive. CI on PR #125 died with "JavaScript heap out of memory" (exit 134, about 3.35 GB) and it reproduced locally (V8 gave up at "3356.8 MB" after 174 of 189 suites; retained heap grew a median 24.8 MB per suite from the first suite). It was not Sentry: the SDK itself grows the heap by 0.2 MB over 300 init / capture / close cycles, and the OBS work only added about ten more app-loading suites. Two causes, fixed in `e8a71b3`: the `express-rate-limit` MemoryStore's repeating 15-minute timer (a real Node timer jest never clears) and the Redis client (one never-quit `ioredis` socket per test file when Redis is on, as in CI). Limiter stores now come from `src/utils/rate-limit-stores.js`, `closeRedisClient()` was added to `src/config/redis.config.js`, and `tests/harness/release-process-resources.js` (jest `setupFilesAfterEnv`) releases both after every test file; it looks each function up and swallows errors, because `access.test.js` mocks `redis.config`. Measured at Node's default heap: 3,302 MB at suite 174 then the crash, against a peak of 321 MB over 190 suites. **Rules for the next agent: a new `rateLimit()` call must pass `store: rateLimitStores.createMemoryStore()` (a source-scan test fails otherwise); any new process-level resource (timer, client, server) that survives a test file must be released in that hook.** GC-reachability tests were tried and dropped as unreliable in one jest process. The CI test step also has `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=6144` as a safety net, and the job timeout is now 60 minutes (`181d54a`); `workerIdleMemoryLimit` does not apply (`--runInBand`).

**Deploy notes (read before deploying).**
- **Migration p022 (OBS-05).** Apply it before the backend that contains OBS-05 goes live: the `BillingEvent` model selects `tenant_id`, so without it every `billing_events` read fails. `node src/scripts/run-platform-migrations.js --dry-run` first, then without the flag. Backup: `mysqldump <platform_db> billing_events > billing_events_pre_p022.sql`. Rollback (deploy the previous build first): `ALTER TABLE billing_events DROP INDEX billing_events_tenant_status, DROP COLUMN tenant_id; DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 22;`. Old rows keep `tenant_id` NULL (no backfill). **I could not verify whether p022 has been applied anywhere.**
- **New environment variables, all optional; nothing changes until one is set.** `METRICS_KEY` (24 or more characters; point the scraper at `/metrics` with `Authorization: Bearer <key>`; unset means a 404) and `METRICS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX`; `SENTRY_DSN`, `SENTRY_RELEASE` (for example the git SHA) and `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT`; `SLOW_QUERY_MS` (for example 200); `LOG_FORMAT` (`json` or `text`) and `LOG_LEVEL`.
- **Logs are JSON in production** from OBS-01 (plain text in development and test; `LOG_FORMAT=text` forces text). Check that any log shipper parses one JSON object per line. `morgan` is no longer used by the app.
- **Alerts need at least one ACTIVE platform admin** to receive the in-app notification, and they arrive at most once per code per hour.
- **Owner action for OBS-04:** set `slow_query_log=1` and `long_query_time=0.2` on the MySQL servers and tick the checklist in `docs/runbooks/slow-queries.md` section 4. Until then OBS-04 stays NEEDS RUNTIME CHECK.
- **NEW-86 (`9c6f710`) changes who receives realtime events:** staff now join the tenant and branch rooms of every organization they belong to, so with NEW-84 / 84b / 85 they receive their branches' messages and ledger updates. Mobile shows no organization label on those (NEW-91).
- No tenant migration in 5A-3.

**What could not be verified in 5A-3.** A real Sentry project (the SDK was exercised with a fake transport); `/metrics` scraped by a real Prometheus; the MySQL slow log on a real server (the runbook SQL ran only on local MySQL 5.7.44); any item on staging or production; whether p022 is applied; and the GitHub CI result of each PR beyond what the owner reported (PR #125 first failed on the timeout and the heap crash, both fixed in the commits listed above).

**Observed and recorded** as NEW-87 to NEW-94 (§13), plus the NEW-83 sleep hypothesis.

**Not done in 5A-3.** PR9 (client Sentry for CMS, web and mobile; needs the DSN), PR12 (OBS-08), dashboards and a metrics scraper, the on-call rota, the MySQL slow log on the servers, and NEW-87 to NEW-94. GLB-03 to GLB-09 and the 5A-1 and 5A-2 leftovers are unchanged.

#### Prompt 5A-4 close-out notes (2026-10-20)

**Scope.** SEC-04, SEC-05, SEC-08, SEC-14, SEC-16, SEC-17, SEC-18, BILL-15 and the recorded open risk SEC-15, in seven PRs: backend #128 to #133 (`accd58c` SEC-05, `25d9a54` SEC-04, `ea29ba2` SEC-08, `d43ba9a` SEC-17, `f9c226a` SEC-16, `0722f15` BILL-15), web #18 (`6ae234f`), CMS #30 (`6190fe7`), #31 (`72f9c52`) and the BILL-15 badge `88778bb` (not seen merged). Decisions R-35 to R-38 (§14) are recorded. **Nothing here was verified on staging or production.**

**How it was verified.** Every PR was failing-first; test counts are in the rows. Full protocol, once, after PR 7, on backend commit `0722f15` (the content of #133) with the Mac on AC power, lid open, `caffeinate`, sleep log checked: backend suite, 200 suites / 1888 tests, three normal runs (Redis on) all green (17:29 to 18:30, about 20 minutes each) plus one `DISABLE_REDIS=true` run, also green (18:30 to 18:51); `pmset -g log` shows no sleep or wake event between 17:00 and 19:10 (the last was 11:36), AC power throughout, lid open. Web: Vitest 95 passed, `npm run build` compiled, `next lint` clean, Playwright against `next start` 15 passed, default Playwright (dev server) 3 passed. CMS (on the BILL-15 branch, so it includes `88778bb`): Vitest 439 passed (60 files), `npm run build` compiled, `next lint` no errors, Playwright against `next start` 9 passed, default Playwright (dev server) 26 passed. Each client suite ran once, as planned.

**Process notes.**
1. The 5A-3 rule held: every new limiter registers its store with the test cleanup hook (`rateLimiters.js` uses `createMemoryStore`), and the existing source guard covers new files.
2. `sharp` was installed from the lockfile in a scratch directory for `linux-x64` (glibc) and `win32-x64` and both binaries resolved; **neither was ever loaded**. Only macOS x64 ran it.
3. The failing-first run of SEC-08 showed the old fallback writing files into `public/uploads` (the point of the fix); those were deleted.
4. Next.js pages cannot export extra components (`next build` fails): the BILL-15 badge is a local component.

**Deploy notes (read before deploying; in this order).**
- **Migration p023** (BILL-15): `node src/scripts/run-platform-migrations.js --dry-run`, then without the flag. New table `billing_plan_price_checks`; nothing existing changes. Rollback (previous build first): `DROP TABLE billing_plan_price_checks; DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 23;`.
- **R2 variables (SEC-08): `R2_ACCOUNT_ID`, `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, `R2_BUCKET`, `R2_PUBLIC_URL` must all be set in production.** Until now a missing value silently stored files on public local disk. From this release every image upload answers 503. Check before deploying.
- **`sharp` and Node:** `sharp` 0.35.5 needs **Node >= 20.9.0** (`engines` raised). After `npm ci` on the Windows host, search the log for `[image-sanitizer] sharp is not available`: if present, uploads still work but are checked by signature only and not re-encoded.
- **Rate limits (SEC-04):** nothing to set. Overrides if a limit hurts real users: `RATE_LIMIT_<NAME>_MAX` and `_WINDOW_S` for LOGIN_IP, LOGIN_ID, REGISTER_IP, REGISTER_ID, OTP_IP, OTP_ID, PWRESET_IP, PWRESET_ID, SEARCH_IP, MUTATION_USER, REVEAL_USER (defaults in `.env.example`). **Check `trust proxy` first:** `app.set('trust proxy', 1)` is right for exactly one proxy in front of the API. With IIS plus Cloudflare, `req.ip` may be a proxy address, and then every user shares one per-IP bucket. Log `req.ip` for a few requests from known client addresses before relying on it; adjust the hop count (or read `CF-Connecting-IP`) if it is wrong.
- **Google (BILL-15):** `GOOGLE_PLAY_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON` and `GOOGLE_PLAY_PACKAGE_NAME` enable the daily check (03:40, `PRICE_VERIFICATION_CRON` to change it). The service account must be allowed to read the app's subscriptions in Play Console. Unset means the job does nothing and every plan shows "admin-attested". Run `node src/scripts/run-price-verification.js` once by hand after setting them.
- **Seeders (SEC-05):** they refuse `NODE_ENV=production`; admin passwords come from `SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD` or a one-time printed value. The four `/system` routes are gone: any deploy habit that called them (`run-install`, `run-pull`) must use the host instead. If any deployed environment ever used the old keys or the demo `TENANT_CONN_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (`0123456789abcdef…`), rotate with `docs/runbooks/secrets-rotation.md`.
- **CI:** a new workflow `secret-scan.yml` (gitleaks, pinned and checksum-verified) scans the commits each push or PR adds. Its first run on GitHub is the first time it runs on Linux.
- **CSP follow-ups (web and CMS):** the policy is Report-Only. Violations are logged by the Next.js server as `[csp-report] {...}`. Read them for a few days across the real pages (gym detail with Maps, Google sign-in, dashboards, the KYC preview), fix the allow-list, then change `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` to `Content-Security-Policy` in each `middleware.ts`. CMS: check a PDF KYC preview in a real browser, then enforce `object-src 'none'` too. HSTS is `max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains` without preload: make sure every subdomain is HTTPS first.
- **Pages are now server-rendered per request** in web and CMS (needed for the nonce); expect a little more CPU on the Node host.
- **Admin Members tab (SEC-17):** contact details are masked; support staff use "Show contact", which writes an audit row. Reveals are limited to 300 per hour per admin.

**What could not be verified in 5A-4.** Anything on staging or production; the real Play Developer API (response shape taken from Google's documentation); `sharp` on Windows and on a Linux runner; Google Maps and Google sign-in under the CSP against the real hosts; the IIS / Cloudflare layer (duplicate or conflicting headers, the client IP); load behaviour of the rate limits (the spec's load test); whether the new GitHub workflow passes; and whether the CMS BILL-15 PR is merged.

**Observed and recorded** as NEW-95 and NEW-96 (§13).

**Not done in 5A-4 (later work).** SEC-15 cookie sessions plus CSRF (its own prompt) and enforcing the CSP; presigned direct-to-R2 uploads and virus scanning (SEC-08, R-36); a Redis store for the rate limits when a second API instance exists; an App Store Connect API key and the Apple price read-back (BILL-15, R-38); the secret manager / KMS (owner); presigned uploads for KYC are not needed (private storage already). GLB-03 to GLB-09 and the 5A-1 / 5A-2 / 5A-3 leftovers are unchanged.

#### UX-12 implementation plan (PLANNED, Prompt 1F, 2026-09-30)

Goal (§8.3.7): the CMS `/gym/team` page is the mobile Team & Access screen on a wide layout. It uses the same endpoints, copy and states, so the same person shows the same effective permissions in both. Nothing new on the backend: the `/team` routes and the permission model already exist (RBAC-03 / RBAC-07).

1. **API layer (CMS).** Add `cms/src/lib/api/team.ts` with exactly the calls the mobile repository makes (`gyms_era/.../team_repository.dart`): `GET /team/meta/roles`, `GET /team/meta/permissions`, `GET /team`, `GET /team/:assignmentId`, `POST /team/invites`, `PATCH /team/:assignmentId` (role/scope), `PUT /team/:assignmentId/permissions` (overrides), `DELETE /team/:assignmentId` (revoke), plus `/approvals`, `/approvals/mine`, `/approvals/:id/approve|reject|cancel`. Always send `X-Tenant-Id` (SEC-02 selector). Note: §8.3.3 describes `PATCH /team/:id/grants { changes, expectedVersion }`; the code and mobile use `PUT /team/:assignmentId/permissions`. Follow the code and mobile, and fix §8.3.3 wording when this is built.
2. **Query keys and invalidation (§5.2 / §5.3).** `['team', tenantId]`, `['team', tenantId, assignmentId]`, `['team-meta', tenantId]`, `['approvals', tenantId, tab]`. Invite, role change, permission save and revoke invalidate `team` and the member; approve/reject invalidate `approvals` and the badge.
3. **Page `/gym/team`.** Left: a member table with the mobile role filter chips and live counts, hiding roles with nobody in them (§8.3.2), plus search. Right: a side sheet for the selected member with (a) role and scope, (b) the 3-choice editor Off / Needs approval / Direct, with preset-only tiers (VIEW / APPROVE / FULL) shown as read-only labels such as "Can approve · from Manager role", which saving never changes (RBAC-01), (c) the before/after diff confirmation before saving, (d) "Revoke access" (keeps the record) and "Restore access" (new assignment). Use the mobile role labels and the error-copy table (§4.2). On `409` (someone else saved first), reload the sheet and say so (RBAC-08).
4. **Invite.** Port `add_team_member_screen.dart` as a dialog: phone or email, role (only roles strictly below the inviter's level; the server re-checks), scope (organization or branches). Show the pending-invite state as mobile does.
5. **Approvals.** A `/gym/approvals` page (or a tab on the team page, whichever the mobile drawer placement maps to) with *Waiting on you* / *Your requests*, the server badge count, approve / reject / cancel.
6. **Navigation.** Replace the sidebar "Staff" entry with "Team & access". "Trainers" stays as the bookable-profile screen (R-14) but links a trainer to a team member instead of creating access. Guard the page with `team.view` from the grants the CMS already loads; hide the actions the user lacks (`team.invite`, `team.role.assign`, `team.permission.override`), while the server stays the authority.
7. **Retire the old path (§0.5).** Remove `/gym/staff` and its `gym.ts` staff calls from the CMS. In the backend, answer `410 Gone` on `/gyms/branches/:id/staff` (POST/DELETE) and `/gyms/staff` (POST/DELETE) one release after the CMS ships, unless the mobile app still calls them (check `gyms_era` first; §3.3 notes the old `/host/admins` and `/host/profile/staff` routes still exist in the app router).
8. **Tests.** Vitest component tests for the sheet (preset labels read-only, diff shows only changed rows, 409 reload) and the chips (counts, empty roles hidden). A Playwright flow: invite → edit a permission → revoke. The §8.3.7 acceptance check as an API-level test: for one person, `GET /team/:id` gives the same effective tiers the app shows.
9. **Size and order.** About 1 prompt of CMS work (Phase 3, with RBAC-01/02/04/05/06/08/09). Backend changes are limited to the step 7 retirement. Screen states (loading / empty / error / forbidden) follow §2.5.

### Phase 7 incident 2026-10-26: approved host's Gyms tab "A database error occurred", no API logs (Claude Code Opus 5.5)

Reported after P2 was deployed (2026-10-26 07:00): an approved host's Gyms & Operations tab shows "A database error occurred" (reference `5bf8b433-bb37-4802-98ff-10baf60168c4`); an unpaid host's tab works; the API log folder has had no new file since 2026-10-16. Branch `fix/g-gyms-tab-tenant-schema` (from origin/main `8c3e748`), not pushed.

Gyms tab calls for an approved host (app `host_gyms_overview_screen.dart:121-131`, `host_providers.dart`): `GET /host/status` (or the `/me/bootstrap` seed), `/host/listings`, `/host/branches`, `/host/branch-quota` (+ `?organizationId=` per organization), `/subscriptions/staff?status=PENDING&limit=1` (errors swallowed), `/notifications/unread-count` (NEW-121: non-fatal), and per branch card `/host/branches/:id/dashboard`. Of the P2 files, `host-status.service.js` reads only the platform DB (same SQL for paid and unpaid hosts), `branch-quota.service.js` reads the tenant DB with `COUNT(*)` only, `context.controller.js` and `discovery.service.js` are not on this tab's tenant-DB path.

| Issue | Root cause (file:line, before) | Pattern reused | Fix | Regression test | Result |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **NEW-124** (P0 until the owner's check; then P1) | Not a P2 code regression. `gym.service.js:269` `Branch.findAll` (GET /host/branches; also the branch dashboard) selects every `Branch.model.js` column, including `currency` (GLB-02, `ebec891`, 2026-10-14). ### NEW-123 reopened 2026-10-27: the first fix did not work on real iisnode (Claude Code Opus 5.5)

Owner evidence: iisnode process 31872 (`api.gymsera.com\wwwroot\server.js`) started 2026-10-26 12:53 server time on `d023bc3` (contains `f457114`, `src/utils/error-journal.js` present), served requests and answered 5xx `database_error`, yet no `iisnode/*-stdout-*.txt` since 2026-10-16 and no file in the `logs` folder. Branch `fix/new-123-iisnode-real` (from origin/main `d023bc3`), not pushed.

Reproduced on the Mac with the real iisnode interceptor (`Azure/iisnode` `src/scripts/interceptor.js` + `logs.template.html`, run as iisnode runs it: `IISNODE_LOGGINGENABLED=1 node interceptor.js <wwwroot>/server.js`) in front of the real `log-redaction` / `logger` / `error-journal`: before the fix, `iisnode/` empty and every line on descriptor 1; with an unwritable journal folder, the journal folder empty too: the production picture.

| Issue | Root cause (file:line, before) | Pattern reused | Fix | Regression test | Result |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **NEW-123** (P1, reopened) | (1) `src/utils/logger.js:25-26` (`f457114`) wrote through `process.stdout` only when `process.stdout.fd !== 1`. The interceptor does not replace `process.stdout`: it assigns `stream.write = stream.end = function ...` on the same object (`interceptor.js`, `intercept()`), and Node always sets `process.stdout.fd = 1`, so the branch never ran and lines still went to `fs.writeSync(1)`. The `f457114` test faked a different stdout object with `fd: null`, which iisnode never does. (2) `src/utils/error-journal.js:71-73` swallowed every write error with no trace, and the console line that could have said so went to descriptor 1 as well, so a journal folder the app pool identity cannot write (most likely; the boot line will say) left nothing anywhere. Exact cause of (2) on the server not proven from here: an unwritable `logs/` and `NODE_ENV` not `production` give the same empty folder; the new boot line tells them apart | OBS-01 `out.write` seam, NEW-123 journal | (1) the logger writes through `process.stdout.write` when it is an own property of `process.stdout` (a host installed it; Node's is on the prototype), else `writeSync(1)` as before (Vercel). (2) A journal write that fails goes to `<os temp>/gymsera-logs` instead, and the failure (folder, error code, fallback) is printed once per folder and code. (3) `boot()` (called near the top of `server.js`) writes `{"event":"boot",pid,node}` to the journal and prints `[ErrorJournal] boot pid=... NODE_ENV=... journal=<file or NOT WRITTEN / off>`, so a deploy can be checked without waiting for a 5xx | be `tests/regression/new-123-error-journal.test.js` (7): iisnode-faithful stdout test (own `write` on the real `process.stdout`, `fd` 1), boot line, unwritable folder falls back and reports once | red 3/7 on `d023bc3` source; green 7/7; real-interceptor harness: writable folder, stdout file has the boot line, journal has boot + 5xx lines; denied folder, stdout file has `cannot write ... EACCES; writing to <temp>/gymsera-logs instead` and the boot line, fallback file has boot + 5xx lines. ~~Status: NOT VERIFIED IN PRODUCTION.~~ **VERIFIED in production 2026-10-28** (section "Phase 7 production verification 2026-10-28"): production's boot line is read on the server |

Tests: backend full suite 228 / 228 suites, 2318 / 2318 tests green (local Docker MySQL 5.7, DISABLE_REDIS=true); related logging suites 16 / 239. Not tested: real IIS / Windows (ACLs, the app pool's temp folder). The interceptor is run under Node 20 on macOS.

Deploy (owner): push and merge `fix/new-123-iisnode-real`, deploy; `server.js` is in `watchedFiles`, so iisnode restarts. No migration. Then on the server: (a) a new `iisnode\<host>-<pid>-stdout-<ts>.txt` must exist with `[ErrorJournal] boot pid=<pid>`; (b) that line names the journal file; open it and find `"event":"boot"` with the same pid. If it says `NOT WRITTEN` or `journal=off`, or the stdout file has `cannot write to ...\logs: EPERM`, grant the app pool identity (`IIS AppPool\<pool name>`) Modify on `wwwroot\logs`, or set `ERROR_LOG_DIR` to a writable folder, then recycle. If `journal=off`, `NODE_ENV` is not `production` in that process. If no stdout file appears at all, send the `iisnode` element of the live `web.config` and the iisnode version.

### NEW-127 / NEW-128 / NEW-126 2026-10-27: a live tenant with no schema_migrations (Claude Code Opus 5.5)

Owner evidence (grid 1 of the NEW-124 check on production): tenant GYM-4814C134 (`gymsera_gym_4814c134`, `tenants.updated_at` 2026-10-24) has no `schema_migrations` and no `branches.currency`; the other 30 tenants have both. Its approved host gets `database_error` on the Gyms tab (NEW-124 mechanism). The SQL check then stopped after grid 1 with no output (NEW-128). Branch be `fix/new-127-tenant-schema-baseline` from origin/main `981304d`, not pushed. Production was not reachable from the agent's machine (local `.env` points at localhost), so the history of this one tenant is not proven; `docs/sql/new-127-tenant-history.sql` settles it.

**How a tenant can be live without migrations (code trace).** A fresh approval cannot do it: the CMS calls `POST /admin/tenants/:id/approve` → `admin.service.js:368 approveTenant` → `tenant-provisioning.service.js processTenantProvisioning`; step 2 `sync({ alter: true })` adds every model column (including `currency`) and step 6 (`stepActivate`) runs every migration (test 1 below is green on the old code). `updated_at` is the row's last change, not the approval date. Every in-repo path that sets ACTIVE: `stepActivate`, `reactivateTenant` (migrates first), `assignTenantSubscription` (`admin.service.js:1077-1081`, SUSPENDED → ACTIVE, no migration), cancelling an account deletion (`account-deletion.service.js:246-248`, back to `statusBeforeDeletion`, no migration). A database with tables but neither `schema_migrations` nor `currency` was built by code older than the runner (2026-09-26, `d7d4179`) and never reached by a run since: `runAllTenantMigrations` and the startup check read `status = 'ACTIVE'` only (`tenant-migration-runner.js:879`, `:970` on `981304d`), so a tenant suspended (or pending deletion) during every run is skipped and then goes live through one of the two paths above. Most likely for GYM-4814C134: provisioned before 2026-09-26, suspended at subscription expiry, renewed on 2026-10-24 (`assignTenantSubscription`). Unproven until the owner runs the history SQL.

| Issue | Root cause | Pattern reused | Fix | Regression test | Result |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **NEW-127** (P0) provisioning / runner | (1) `stepActivate` (`tenant-provisioning.service.js:716-726`) set ACTIVE even when a migration returned `skipped`, so provisioning could finish below target. (2) The runner and the startup check covered ACTIVE tenants only (above). (3) The runner, given a database with tables but no `schema_migrations`, replayed every migration from 001 (backfills, collation conversion) and reported success | tenant migration runner, FLOW-02 step machine (failure leaves APPROVED at the last step; Resume) | Step 6 checks `missingTenantMigrations()` after the run and throws `tenant_schema_incomplete` naming the migrations: the tenant stays APPROVED at SUBSCRIPTION_LINKED with `provisioningError`, Resume retries. Runner and check cover `MIGRATABLE_TENANT_STATUSES` = ACTIVE, SUSPENDED, PENDING_DELETION. The runner refuses an un-baselined database (dry run and live), touches nothing, and names `repair-tenant-schema.js --tenant <code>`. The check reports `tenantCode`, `hasMigrationTable`, missing versions and unreadable tenants | be `tests/regression/new-127-tenant-schema-complete.test.js` (7): CMS approve over HTTP ends with versions 1..19 and `currency`; a skipping migration keeps the tenant APPROVED, Resume finishes it; SUSPENDED and PENDING_DELETION tenants at v18 are migrated; legacy database refused with nothing written; check output; `missingTenantMigrations` | red 6/7 on `981304d` (the fresh approve was already green); green 7/7; commit `1550daf` |
| **NEW-127** repair | No way to bring a database without `schema_migrations` to target without replaying everything | the runner's own `up` functions and skip rules; `run-sql-check` / orphan-drop CLI conventions (dry run default, `--confirm <exact name>`) | `src/database/tenant-schema-baseline.js`: a read-only probe per migration (present / missing / conflict, mirroring each `up`'s skip rule; a test fails if a migration has no probe). Refuses on: existing `schema_migrations`, no `branches` / `gyms`, any conflict, or a model table / column no migration adds (NEW-126). Apply: create the table, record the present ones (no DDL), run only the missing ones through `runTenantMigrations`, verify 1..19. `src/scripts/repair-tenant-schema.js --tenant <code> [--apply --confirm <code>]`, exit 0 / 2 refused / 1 incomplete | be `tests/regression/new-127-repair-tenant-schema.test.js` (13): probe coverage; all present after a full run; dry run changes nothing (schema fingerprint); confirm guard; apply runs 019 only, records 1..19, keeps rows, `currency` = PKR; a middle gap (008) runs too; five refusals with the schema unchanged; real CLI ends on its own | red: module missing on `c01873d`; green 13/13; commit `c7344ed` |

### NEW-127 production evidence 2026-10-27: the tenant was made by a build from before 2026-09-26 (Claude Code Opus 5.5)

Owner evidence (history SQL on production): GYM-4814C134 was created 2026-10-24 07:18 and approved 10:43:44. All 24 of its tables were created within 2 seconds of the approval (information_schema; the server clock is 5 hours behind). It was the only gym created or approved since 2026-10-24. It ended `status` ACTIVE, `provisioning_state` NULL, `provisioning_error` NULL, with no `schema_migrations`. **This disproves the guess above (suspended, then renewed): a fresh approval on 10/24 built the tables without migrations and still ended ACTIVE.** The owner has since repaired it with `repair-tenant-schema.js`; it is at v19, and `run-tenant-migrations.js --check` exits 0 for all 31 tenants.

**Which code did this (git; there is no deploy record, no tag and no deploy workflow, so git cannot say what was deployed).** main on 2026-10-24 (`b3df7ff`, and every commit since `d326909` FLOW-02 on 2026-10-01) cannot produce this state. `approveTenant` writes `provisioning_state = REQUESTED` before any step (`admin.service.js:417-421` at `b3df7ff`), every step records its own state, and step 6 runs every migration. Since `d7d4179` (2026-09-26), provisioning calls `runTenantMigrations` and has no catch around it, so a failure would have kept the tenant from ACTIVE. The build just before `d7d4179` (`f083c0f`, 2026-09-26 18:54) matches every observation. `processTenantProvisioning` there runs `tenantSequelize.sync({ force: false, alter: true })` (step 6), has no migration step, and sets `status: ACTIVE` (step 9); the Tenant model has no `provisioningState`, so the column stays NULL. Syncing its tenant models makes exactly 24 tables with no `branches.currency`; today's code makes 28, and 29 with `schema_migrations` (both counted on local MySQL). Tenant models had 24 tables from 2026-09-15 to 2026-09-28, so the running build was cut from main between 2026-09-15 and `d7d4179`. The other 30 tenants have `schema_migrations` because the deploy runner (`run-tenant-migrations.js`, newer code on disk) migrated every ACTIVE tenant, while the code serving requests was older. Not proven, and only the server can tell: why the API process ran a September build on 10/24. Lead: `web.config` has `watchedFiles="web.config;server.js"`, so iisnode reloads only when one of those two files changes; files copied under `src/` are not picked up until the process restarts. Second lead: the IIS site points at a different folder from the one deploys copy into. The API log folder having no new file since 2026-10-16 (NEW-123) fits both.

| Issue | Root cause | Pattern reused | Fix | Regression test | Result |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **NEW-127** provisioning, production cause | The API on 10/24 ran a build from before `d7d4179`: `tenant-provisioning.service.js` at `f083c0f` syncs 24 models, runs no migration and sets ACTIVE with no `provisioning_state`. Today's provisioning is correct (fixed and tested in `1550daf`) | harness `setupTestDatabases` / `getAdminConnection` / `factories`, `admin.service#approveTenant`; `run-sql-check.js` (NEW-124) | No code change to provisioning. Test of the state a fresh approval leaves behind, written with only what `f083c0f` had, so it runs there too. `docs/sql/new-127-fresh-approval-check.sql`: the same check for production after every deploy (PASS / FAIL on the gym approved last, plus its recorded migrations through PREPARE of a SELECT) | `tests/regression/new-127-fresh-approval-invariant.test.js` (8) | Red 6/8 on `f083c0f`: four state tests fail on the old behaviour itself (`schema_migrations` missing; migration list missing; `branches.currency` missing; `provisioning_state` missing), and the two check-script tests fail because the script does not exist there. The ACTIVE test and the model-tables test pass there; the model-tables test passes because it compares with that build's own 24 models. Green 8/8 on main (`70cdd72` + this commit) |
| **NEW-129** (P3, new) p017 skipped in CI | `TenantSubscription.model.js:171-177` declares `duplicate_billing` NOT NULL DEFAULT false; p017 (`platform-migrations.js:689-697`) only accepts `tinyint(1)` NULL. The harness runs `sync({ force: true })` (`tests/harness/test-db.js:175`) before the platform migrations, so p017 always skips and v17 is never recorded on test databases | — | Not fixed (owner asked for an explanation only). Production check: `docs/sql/new-129-p017-platform-check.sql` | — | — |
| **NEW-130** (P1) nothing shows which build is live | `/health` returned a fixed `version: '1.1.0'` (`src/routes/index.js:29-39`); there was no commit id anywhere at runtime, so a stale process looked the same as a fresh one | the NEW-123 boot line (`error-journal.js#boot`), the `/health` route | `src/utils/build-info.js`: commit and build time read once at startup from GYMSERA_BUILD_COMMIT / GYMSERA_BUILD_TIME, else `build-info.json`, else the app folder's own git checkout (HEAD and its commit time; a folder that is only inside another repo does not count), else `unknown`. `/health` returns `build { commit, builtAt, source }` and `startedAt`. The boot line adds `commit=`, `built=`, `started=` and `platformDb=` | `tests/regression/new-130-build-info.test.js` (7) | Red 2/7 on main's route and boot line (the 5 reader tests need the new module), green 7/7. be `a7892b9` |

The local development `.env` checked on 2026-10-27 has exactly that pair. Nothing compared staging's database with production's, so a staging API (other code) could approve gyms into production.
